[EVENT: YEM81088]
Date: 2023-07-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Taizz - Al Ardi
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Yemen) [Political militia]
Actor2: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Masdar; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 July 2023, unidentified gunmen clashed with IRG guards during an Eid festival in Ash Shuhada Square in Taizz - Al Ardi (Salh) injuring 2 people. A fistfight was followed by gunfire after participants refused to distribute posters of Tareq Saleh which prompted gunmen to intervene and clash with IRG guards.

[EVENT: YEM81086]
Date: 2023-07-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Harib Junction
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Fujayh Tribal Militia (Yemen) [Identity militia]
Actor2: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council [State forces]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Yemen Data Project; Yemen Press Agency [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 July 2023, IRG Islah-affiliated road security forces clashed with Al Fujayh tribesmen in Harib Junction (Marib) killing 2 fighters and injuring 3 others of both sides over 1 and 2 July. 2 fatalities split between 2 dates. The clash came after a previous round of clashes between Al Fujayh tribesmen and IRG forces after IRG forces killed 1 tribal sheikh on 23 June. Tribal mediation put an end to the clashes.

[EVENT: YEM81114]
Date: 2023-07-03
Country: Yemen | Location: Ataq
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Shabwani Defense Forces [State forces]
Actor2: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - Special Security Forces [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Crater Sky; Yemen Data Project; YNP [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 July 2023, STC Shabwani Defense forces clashed with IRG police patrol forces by Ataq New Hospital as well as near the Central Bank in Ataq city (Shabwah) injuring 3 from the police patrol including its commander -- Captain Ahmad Habtur -- and 2 from the SDF. The clash erupted after police patrol forces escorting the convoy of the Central Bank director in Shabwah Governorate refused to hand over their weapons at a SDF checkpoint by Ataq New Hospital leading SDF to open fire at the convoy. The SDF then stormed the Central Bank's building in Ataq with 3 armored vehicles leading to further clashes between the SDF and the police patrol and bank guards.
